## Complete scripts/test-channels-local.sh

Original bytes/SHA binding: {"path": "scripts/test-channels-local.sh", "bytes": 13918, "sha256": "1ab8c3da8e7d05d7c25057356fa3802a3671a86288798e87140d9b8f86ede06b"}

```
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

ROOT_DIR="$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
TARGET="${1:-all}"
if (($# > 1)); then
  printf 'Usage: %s [api|api-instagram|web|browser|all]\n' "$0" >&2
  exit 2
fi
case "$TARGET" in
  api|api-instagram|web|browser|all) ;;
  *) printf 'Usage: %s [api|api-instagram|web|browser|all]\n' "$0" >&2; exit 2 ;;
esac

CHANNELS_DOCKER_TRANSPORT="${CHANNELS_DOCKER_TRANSPORT:-windows}"
case "$CHANNELS_DOCKER_TRANSPORT" in
  windows)
    command -v docker.exe >/dev/null || { echo 'Windows Docker CLI (docker.exe) is required.' >&2; exit 1; }
    command -v wslpath >/dev/null || { echo 'wslpath is required to bind the principal checkout.' >&2; exit 1; }
    DOCKER=(docker.exe)
    host_path() { wslpath -w "$1"; }
    bind_host_path() { wslpath -w "$1"; }
    ;;
  linux)
    command -v docker >/dev/null || { echo 'Linux Docker CLI (docker) is required for the linux transport.' >&2; exit 1; }
    DOCKER=(docker)
    host_path() { printf '%s' "$1"; }
    bind_host_path() {
      local path="$1"
      if [[ -n "${WSL_DISTRO_NAME:-}" ]]; then
        if [[ "${DOCKER_HOST:-}" != "unix:///mnt/wsl/docker-desktop/shared-sockets/guest-services/docker.sock" ]]; then
          echo 'WSL Linux transport requires the verified Docker Desktop shared socket.' >&2
          return 1
        fi
        if [[ "$path" =~ ^/mnt/([a-z])/(.+)$ ]]; then
          printf '/run/desktop/mnt/host/%s/%s' "${BASH_REMATCH[1]}" "${BASH_REMATCH[2]}"
        else
          printf 'Unsupported WSL bind source path for Docker Desktop: %s\n' "$path" >&2
          return 1
        fi
      else
        printf '%s' "$path"
      fi
    }
    ;;
  *) printf 'Unsupported CHANNELS_DOCKER_TRANSPORT: %s\n' "$CHANNELS_DOCKER_TRANSPORT" >&2; exit 2 ;;
esac
CHANNELS_SOURCE_WINDOWS="$(bind_host_path "$ROOT_DIR")"
CHANNELS_EXPECTED_API_PACKAGE_SHA256="$(sha256sum "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/package.json" | cut -d ' ' -f 1)"
CHANNELS_EXPECTED_WEB_PACKAGE_SHA256="$(sha256sum "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/package.json" | cut -d ' ' -f 1)"

# Keep host inputs as a positive inventory. Do not discover dotenv files with
# find/glob over either application tree: those trees can contain private
# configuration and local-only data. The exact public bind paths are listed
# below; only api-app/.env.example is mounted, while .env and .env.testing are
# supplied inside the validation container by the Compose config masks below.

cd "$ROOT_DIR"
temp_env="$(mktemp)"
temp_compose_overlay=""
chmod 600 "$temp_env"
cat > "$temp_env" <<EOF
CHANNELS_SOURCE_WINDOWS=$CHANNELS_SOURCE_WINDOWS
SESSION_SECRET=channels-validation-session-only
INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN=channels-validation-internal-only
POSTGRES_PASSWORD=channels-validation-postgres-only
EVOLUTION_POSTGRES_PASSWORD=channels-validation-evolution-postgres-only
EVOLUTION_AUTHENTICATION_API_KEY=channels-validation-evolution-api-key-only
EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET=validation-evolution-header-secret
CHANNELS_EXPECTED_API_PACKAGE_SHA256=$CHANNELS_EXPECTED_API_PACKAGE_SHA256
CHANNELS_EXPECTED_WEB_PACKAGE_SHA256=$CHANNELS_EXPECTED_WEB_PACKAGE_SHA256
EOF
add_bind_path() {
  local key="$1" path="$2"
  local relative_path="${path#"$ROOT_DIR"/}"
  case "$relative_path" in
    .env|.env.testing|api-app/.env|api-app/.env.testing|api-app/.local-files|api-app/.local-files/*|web-app/.env|web-app/.env.*)
      printf 'Refusing excluded private configuration/data path: %s\n' "$relative_path" >&2
      exit 1
      ;;
  esac
  case "$relative_path" in
    api-app/.git|api-app/src|api-app/test|api-app/prisma|api-app/package.json|api-app/package-lock.json|api-app/.env.example|api-app/.dockerignore|api-app/.gitignore|api-app/docker/entrypoint.sh|api-app/Dockerfile|api-app/eslint.config.mjs|api-app/nest-cli.json|api-app/railway.json|api-app/tsconfig.json|api-app/tsconfig.build.json|api-app/vitest.config.ts|api-app/vitest.config.e2e.ts|api-app/vitest.config.cov.ts|web-app/.git|web-app/src|web-app/e2e|web-app/public|web-app/package.json|web-app/package-lock.json|web-app/index.html|web-app/playwright.config.ts|web-app/tsconfig.json|web-app/vite.config.ts|web-app/railway.json|web-app/.gitignore|web-app/.dockerignore|web-app/Dockerfile|web-app/docker/40-config-js.sh|web-app/nginx/default.conf.template) ;;
    *) printf 'Refusing path outside the public bind inventory: %s\n' "$relative_path" >&2; exit 1 ;;
  esac
  [[ -e "$path" ]] || { printf 'Expected principal source path is missing: %s\n' "${path#"$ROOT_DIR"/}" >&2; exit 1; }
  printf '%s=%s\n' "$key" "$(bind_host_path "$path")" >> "$temp_env"
}
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_GIT_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/.git"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_SRC_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/src"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_TEST_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/test"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_PRISMA_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/prisma"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_PACKAGE_JSON_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/package.json"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_PACKAGE_LOCK_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/package-lock.json"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_ENV_EXAMPLE_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/.env.example"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_DOCKERIGNORE_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/.dockerignore"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_GITIGNORE_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/.gitignore"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_ENTRYPOINT_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/docker/entrypoint.sh"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_DOCKERFILE_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/Dockerfile"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_ESLINT_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/eslint.config.mjs"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_NEST_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/nest-cli.json"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_RAILWAY_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/railway.json"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_TSCONFIG_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/tsconfig.json"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_TSCONFIG_BUILD_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/tsconfig.build.json"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_VITEST_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/vitest.config.ts"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_VITEST_E2E_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/vitest.config.e2e.ts"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_VITEST_COV_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/vitest.config.cov.ts"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_WEB_GIT_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/.git"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_WEB_SRC_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/src"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_WEB_E2E_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/e2e"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_WEB_PUBLIC_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/public"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_WEB_PACKAGE_JSON_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/package.json"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_WEB_PACKAGE_LOCK_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/package-lock.json"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_WEB_INDEX_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/index.html"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_WEB_PLAYWRIGHT_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/playwright.config.ts"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_WEB_TSCONFIG_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/tsconfig.json"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_WEB_VITE_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/vite.config.ts"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_WEB_RAILWAY_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/railway.json"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_WEB_GITIGNORE_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/.gitignore"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_WEB_DOCKERIGNORE_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/.dockerignore"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_WEB_DOCKERFILE_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/Dockerfile"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_WEB_CONFIG_JS_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/docker/40-config-js.sh"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_WEB_NGINX_TEMPLATE_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/nginx/default.conf.template"
trap 'rm -f "$temp_env" "$temp_compose_overlay"' EXIT
COMPOSE=("${DOCKER[@]}" compose --project-name leadshug_channels_validation \
  --env-file "$(host_path "$ROOT_DIR/.env.example")" --env-file "$(host_path "$temp_env")" \
  -f "$(host_path "$ROOT_DIR/docker-compose.yml")" -f "$(host_path "$ROOT_DIR/docker-compose.channels.yml")" --profile validation)
if [[ "$CHANNELS_DOCKER_TRANSPORT" == linux && -n "${WSL_DISTRO_NAME:-}" ]]; then
  CHANNELS_API_ENV_EXAMPLE_WINDOWS="$(bind_host_path "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/.env.example")"
  CHANNELS_ROOT_ENV_EXAMPLE_WINDOWS="$(bind_host_path "$ROOT_DIR/.env.example")"
  yaml_scalar() {
    local value="$1"
    value="${value//\\/\\\\}"
    value="${value//\"/\\\"}"
    value="${value//\$/\$\$}"
    printf '"%s"' "$value"
  }
  temp_compose_overlay="$(mktemp)"
  chmod 600 "$temp_compose_overlay"
  {
    printf 'configs:\n'
    printf '  validation_api_env_mask:\n    file: %s\n' "$(yaml_scalar "$CHANNELS_API_ENV_EXAMPLE_WINDOWS")"
    printf '  validation_root_env_mask:\n    file: %s\n' "$(yaml_scalar "$CHANNELS_ROOT_ENV_EXAMPLE_WINDOWS")"
    printf '  validation_root_env_testing_mask:\n    file: %s\n' "$(yaml_scalar "$CHANNELS_ROOT_ENV_EXAMPLE_WINDOWS")"
  } > "$temp_compose_overlay"
  COMPOSE+=(-f "$temp_compose_overlay")
fi

resource_conflicts=()
capture_inventory() {
  local name="$1"; shift
  local output
  if ! output="$("${DOCKER[@]}" "$@")"; then
    printf 'Docker %s inventory failed; validation namespace state is unknown.\n' "$name" >&2
    exit 1
  fi
  printf '%s' "$output"
}
containers_inventory="$(capture_inventory 'container' ps -a --format '{{.Names}}')"
volumes_inventory="$(capture_inventory 'volume' volume ls --format '{{.Name}}')"
networks_inventory="$(capture_inventory 'network' network ls --format '{{.Name}}')"
while IFS= read -r resource_name; do
  if [[ "$resource_name" == leadshug_channels_validation_* || "$resource_name" == leadshug_channels_validation-* ]]; then
    resource_conflicts+=("container:$resource_name")
  fi
done <<< "$containers_inventory"
while IFS= read -r resource_name; do
  [[ "$resource_name" == leadshug_channels_validation_* ]] && resource_conflicts+=("volume:$resource_name")
done <<< "$volumes_inventory"
while IFS= read -r resource_name; do
  [[ "$resource_name" == leadshug_channels_validation_* ]] && resource_conflicts+=("network:$resource_name")
done <<< "$networks_inventory"
if ((${#resource_conflicts[@]})); then
  echo 'Refusing any pre-existing validation namespace resource, regardless of labels.' >&2
  printf '%s\n' "${resource_conflicts[@]}" >&2
  exit 1
fi

started=0
cleanup() {
  local status=$?
  trap - EXIT INT TERM
  if [[ "$started" == 1 ]]; then
    if ! "${COMPOSE[@]}" down --volumes --remove-orphans; then
      echo 'Validation cleanup failed; disposable namespace may require inspection.' >&2
      status=1
    fi
  fi
  rm -f "$temp_env"
  rm -f "$temp_compose_overlay"
  exit "$status"
}
trap cleanup EXIT
trap 'exit 130' INT
trap 'exit 143' TERM
"${COMPOSE[@]}" config --quiet
if [[ "${CHANNELS_CONFIG_ONLY:-0}" == 1 ]]; then
  echo 'Validation Compose config is valid; no resources were created.'
  exit 0
fi
started=1

RUNNER_SCRIPT='set -eu
target="$1"
printf "Runner Node: "
node --version
apt-get update -qq
DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends openssl libssl3 ca-certificates bash git python3 python3-yaml nginx >/dev/null
python3 /workspace/scripts/check-channels-ci-parity.py --self-test
rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
printf "OpenSSL: "
openssl version
ldconfig -p | grep -q "libssl.so.3"
test -f /workspace/api-app/.env
cmp -s /workspace/api-app/.env /workspace/api-app/.env.example || { echo "API dotenv mask mismatch." >&2; exit 1; }
for private_path in /workspace/.env /workspace/.env.testing; do
  test -f "$private_path"
  cmp -s "$private_path" /workspace/.env.example || { echo "Root dotenv mask mismatch." >&2; exit 1; }
done
bound_sha="$(sha256sum /workspace/api-app/package.json | cut -d " " -f 1)"
test "$bound_sha" = "$CHANNELS_EXPECTED_API_PACKAGE_SHA256" || { echo "Source bind checksum mismatch before npm." >&2; exit 1; }
bound_web_sha="$(sha256sum /workspace/web-app/package.json | cut -d " " -f 1)"
test "$bound_web_sha" = "$CHANNELS_EXPECTED_WEB_PACKAGE_SHA256" || { echo "Web source bind checksum mismatch before npm." >&2; exit 1; }
test "$(git -c safe.directory=/workspace -C /workspace rev-parse --show-toplevel)" = /workspace
echo "Principal API/Web source binds and API/root dotenv masks verified before npm."
cd /workspace/api-app
npm ci
prisma_info="$(npx prisma -v)"
printf "%s\n" "$prisma_info"
printf "%s\n" "$prisma_info" | grep -q "debian-openssl-3.0.x" || { echo "Prisma did not detect the supported OpenSSL 3 engine." >&2; exit 1; }
npm run prisma:generate
cd /workspace/web-app
npm ci
case "$target" in
  api|api-instagram|all)
    cd /workspace/api-app
    npm run prisma:migrate
    npx prisma migrate status
    npm run lint
    npm run build
    npm test
    if [ "$target" = api-instagram ]; then
      npm run test:e2e -- test/instagram*.e2e-spec.ts
    else
      npm run test:e2e
    fi
    ;;
esac
case "$target" in
  web|all)
    cd /workspace/web-app
    npm run build
    npm test
    ;;
esac
case "$target" in
  browser|all)
    cd /workspace/api-app
    if [ "$target" = browser ]; then
      echo "Building API before starting browser fixture services."
      npm run build
    fi
    npm run prisma:migrate
    npx prisma migrate status
    cd /workspace/web-app
    npx playwright install --with-deps chromium
    channels_emit_failure_alerts() {
      context_path="$1"
      if [ -f "$context_path" ]; then
        printf "%s\n" "CHANNELS_TEST_FAILURE_ALERT_CONTEXT"
        grep -E "^[[:space:]]*-[[:space:]]*alert([[:space:]:]|$)|^[[:space:]]*-.*role[[:space:]]*=[[:space:]].?alert.?([[:space:]>]|$)" "$context_path" | head -c 4096 || :
      fi
    }
    channels_test_failure_diagnostics() {
      original_status=$?
      trap - 0
      if [ "$original_status" -ne 0 ]; then
        channels_emit_failure_alerts /workspace/web-app/test-results/instagram-channel-Instagra-e3dee-recovery-and-operator-scope/error-context.md
        channels_emit_failure_alerts /workspace/web-app/test-results/timeline-timeline-da-inbox-fcb4b-agens-em-mais-de-uma-rodada/error-context.md
      fi
      exit "$original_status"
    }
    trap channels_test_failure_diagnostics 0
    npm run test:tela -- --trace off
    ;;
esac'

"${COMPOSE[@]}" run --rm -e CHANNELS_EXPECTED_API_PACKAGE_SHA256="$CHANNELS_EXPECTED_API_PACKAGE_SHA256" validation-runner sh -ec "$RUNNER_SCRIPT" channels-validation-runner "$TARGET"

```


## Complete docker-compose.channels.yml

Original bytes/SHA binding: {"path": "docker-compose.channels.yml", "bytes": 21180, "sha256": "9978e713ce190ad20b6bddb0ecf0b469d068c0e43bb4eaf50aa630ec03aea235"}

```
services:
  postgres:
    ports: !reset []
    labels:
      com.leadshug.channels.owner: number-onboarding

  api:
    ports: !reset []
    environment:
      NODE_ENV: ${CHANNELS_NODE_ENV:-development}
      WEB_ORIGIN: ${CHANNELS_LOCAL_ORIGIN:-http://localhost:${CHANNELS_GATEWAY_HOST_PORT:-8083}}
      LEADSHUG_API_URL: ""
    labels:
      com.leadshug.channels.owner: number-onboarding

  web:
    ports: !reset []
    environment:
      LEADSHUG_API_URL: ""
    labels:
      com.leadshug.channels.owner: number-onboarding

  gateway:
    image: nginx:alpine
    profiles: [channels-local]
    depends_on:
      api:
        condition: service_healthy
      web:
        condition: service_started
    ports:
      - "127.0.0.1:${CHANNELS_GATEWAY_HOST_PORT:-8083}:80"
    volumes:
      - ./docker/local-gateway.conf.template:/etc/nginx/templates/default.conf.template:ro
    labels:
      com.leadshug.channels.owner: number-onboarding
    restart: unless-stopped

  evolution-postgres:
    image: postgres:16
    profiles: [channels-local]
    environment:
      POSTGRES_DB: ${EVOLUTION_POSTGRES_DB:-evolution}
      POSTGRES_USER: ${EVOLUTION_POSTGRES_USER:-evolution}
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: ${EVOLUTION_POSTGRES_PASSWORD:?Set EVOLUTION_POSTGRES_PASSWORD in .env.testing}
    volumes:
      - evolution_pg_data:/var/lib/postgresql/data
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "pg_isready -U $$POSTGRES_USER -d $$POSTGRES_DB"]
      interval: 5s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 20
    labels:
      com.leadshug.channels.owner: number-onboarding
    restart: unless-stopped

  evolution-redis:
    image: redis:7.4-alpine
    profiles: [channels-local]
    command: ["redis-server", "--appendonly", "yes"]
    volumes:
      - evolution_redis_data:/data
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD", "redis-cli", "ping"]
      interval: 5s
      timeout: 3s
      retries: 20
    labels:
      com.leadshug.channels.owner: number-onboarding
    restart: unless-stopped

  evolution:
    image: evoapicloud/evolution-api:v2.3.7@sha256:1bd8afc4a6cf48822e6cf02469aeae7bd35a12a6b616eacd1291926307f4d339
    profiles: [channels-local]
    depends_on:
      evolution-postgres:
        condition: service_healthy
      evolution-redis:
        condition: service_healthy
    environment:
      SERVER_NAME: evolution
      SERVER_TYPE: http
      SERVER_PORT: "8080"
      SERVER_URL: ${EVOLUTION_SERVER_URL:-http://evolution:8080}
      SERVER_DISABLE_DOCS: "true"
      DATABASE_ENABLED: "true"
      DATABASE_PROVIDER: postgresql
      DATABASE_CONNECTION_URI: postgresql://${EVOLUTION_POSTGRES_USER:-evolution}:${EVOLUTION_POSTGRES_PASSWORD:?Set EVOLUTION_POSTGRES_PASSWORD in .env.testing}@evolution-postgres:5432/${EVOLUTION_POSTGRES_DB:-evolution}?schema=public
      DATABASE_CONNECTION_CLIENT_NAME: leadshug_channels_local
      DATABASE_SAVE_DATA_INSTANCE: "true"
      DATABASE_SAVE_DATA_NEW_MESSAGE: "true"
      DATABASE_SAVE_MESSAGE_UPDATE: "true"
      DATABASE_SAVE_DATA_CONTACTS: "true"
      DATABASE_SAVE_DATA_CHATS: "true"
      DATABASE_SAVE_DATA_HISTORIC: "true"
      DATABASE_SAVE_DATA_LABELS: "true"
      AUTHENTICATION_API_KEY: ${EVOLUTION_AUTHENTICATION_API_KEY:?Set EVOLUTION_AUTHENTICATION_API_KEY in .env.testing}
      CACHE_REDIS_ENABLED: "true"
      CACHE_REDIS_URI: redis://evolution-redis:6379/6
      CACHE_REDIS_PREFIX_KEY: leadshug_channels_local
      CACHE_REDIS_SAVE_INSTANCES: "true"
      CACHE_LOCAL_ENABLED: "false"
      LOG_LEVEL: ${EVOLUTION_LOG_LEVEL:-ERROR,WARN}
      LOG_COLOR: "false"
    volumes:
      - evolution_instances:/evolution/instances
    expose:
      - "8080"
    labels:
      com.leadshug.channels.owner: number-onboarding
    restart: unless-stopped

  evolution-header-probe:
    image: node:22.23.3-bookworm-slim
    profiles: [channels-probe]
    entrypoint: ["node", "-e"]
    command:
      - |
        const http = require('node:http');
        const { randomUUID } = require('node:crypto');
        const os = require('node:os');
        const port = 8091;
        const receiverIp = Object.values(os.networkInterfaces()).flat().find(address => address && address.family === 'IPv4' && !address.internal)?.address;
        let callbacks = [];
        const server = http.createServer((req, res) => {
          const chunks = [];
          let bytes = 0;
          req.on('data', chunk => { bytes += chunk.length; if (bytes <= 1048576) chunks.push(chunk); });
          req.on('end', () => {
            const headerMatches = req.headers['x-leadshug-webhook-secret'] === process.env.PROBE_EXPECTED_HEADER;
            let event = 'other';
            try {
              const parsed = JSON.parse(Buffer.concat(chunks).toString('utf8'));
              event = typeof parsed.event === 'string' ? parsed.event.toLowerCase() : 'other';
            } catch {}
            const status = headerMatches ? 200 : 403;
            callbacks.push({ headerMatches, event, status });
            console.log(JSON.stringify({ method: req.method, path: req.url === '/probe-callback' ? '/probe-callback' : 'other', event, headerMatches, status }));
            res.writeHead(status).end();
          });
        });
        server.listen(port, '0.0.0.0', async () => {
          const base = 'http://evolution:8080';
          const headers = { 'content-type': 'application/json', apikey: process.env.EVOLUTION_AUTHENTICATION_API_KEY };
          const makeProbe = async (wrong) => {
            const name = 'leadshug-cp17-' + randomUUID();
            const expected = process.env.PROBE_EXPECTED_HEADER;
            const response = await fetch(base + '/instance/create', {
              method: 'POST', headers,
              body: JSON.stringify({ instanceName: name, integration: 'WHATSAPP-BAILEYS', qrcode: false,
                webhook: { enabled: true, events: ['INSTANCE_CREATE'], url: 'http://' + receiverIp + ':8091/probe-callback', headers: { 'x-leadshug-webhook-secret': wrong ? 'deliberately-wrong' : expected }, byEvents: false, base64: false } })
            });
            await response.arrayBuffer();
            const wanted = !wrong;
            const until = Date.now() + 15000;
            while (Date.now() < until && !callbacks.some(c => c.headerMatches === wanted && c.event === 'instance.create')) await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 100));
            const received = callbacks.find(c => c.headerMatches === wanted && c.event === 'instance.create');
            let instanceObservedBeforeDelete = false;
            try {
              const listed = await fetch(base + '/instance/fetchInstances', { headers: { apikey: process.env.EVOLUTION_AUTHENTICATION_API_KEY }, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(2000) });
              const instances = listed.ok ? await listed.json() : null;
              instanceObservedBeforeDelete = Array.isArray(instances) && instances.some(instance => instance?.instance?.instanceName === name || instance?.instanceName === name || instance?.name === name);
            } catch {}
            const del = await fetch(base + '/instance/delete/' + encodeURIComponent(name), { method: 'DELETE', headers, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000) });
            await del.arrayBuffer();
            let removed = false;
            const cleanupUntil = Date.now() + 10000;
            while (Date.now() < cleanupUntil) {
              try {
                const listed = await fetch(base + '/instance/fetchInstances', { headers: { apikey: process.env.EVOLUTION_AUTHENTICATION_API_KEY }, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(2000) });
                if (!listed.ok) throw new Error('list_failed');
                const instances = await listed.json();
                if (!Array.isArray(instances)) throw new Error('invalid_list');
                const stillPresent = instances.some(instance => instance?.instance?.instanceName === name || instance?.instanceName === name || instance?.name === name);
                if (!stillPresent) { removed = true; break; }
              } catch {}
              await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 250));
            }
            return { createStatus: response.status, callbackReceived: !!received, callbackStatusExpected: !!received && received.status === (wrong ? 403 : 200), instanceObservedBeforeDelete, deleteStatus: del.status, removed };
          };
          try {
            const correct = await makeProbe(false);
            callbacks = [];
            const wrong = await makeProbe(true);
            console.log(JSON.stringify({ correct, wrong }));
            if (correct.createStatus < 200 || correct.createStatus >= 300 || !correct.callbackReceived || !correct.callbackStatusExpected || !correct.instanceObservedBeforeDelete || correct.deleteStatus < 200 || correct.deleteStatus >= 300 || wrong.createStatus < 200 || wrong.createStatus >= 300 || !wrong.callbackReceived || !wrong.callbackStatusExpected || !wrong.instanceObservedBeforeDelete || wrong.deleteStatus < 200 || wrong.deleteStatus >= 300 || !correct.removed || !wrong.removed) process.exitCode = 1;
          } catch {
            console.log(JSON.stringify({ success: false, error: 'probe_failed' }));
            process.exitCode = 1;
          } finally { server.close(); }
        });
    environment:
      EVOLUTION_AUTHENTICATION_API_KEY: ${EVOLUTION_AUTHENTICATION_API_KEY:?Set EVOLUTION_AUTHENTICATION_API_KEY in .env.testing}
      PROBE_EXPECTED_HEADER: leadshug-cp21-fictitious-provider-probe-header
    networks: [default]
    labels:
      com.leadshug.channels.owner: number-onboarding
    restart: "no"

  validation-postgres:
    image: postgres:16
    profiles: [validation]
    environment:
      POSTGRES_DB: leadshug_timeline_channels_ci
      POSTGRES_USER: channels_validation
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: channels_validation_only
    volumes:
      - validation_pg_data:/var/lib/postgresql/data
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "pg_isready -U $$POSTGRES_USER -d $$POSTGRES_DB"]
      interval: 5s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 20
    networks: [validation]
    labels:
      com.leadshug.channels.owner: number-onboarding-validation

  validation-runner:
    image: node:22.23.3-bookworm-slim
    profiles: [validation]
    depends_on:
      validation-postgres:
        condition: service_healthy
    working_dir: /workspace
    environment:
      DATABASE_URL: postgresql://channels_validation:channels_validation_only@validation-postgres:5432/leadshug_timeline_channels_ci?schema=public
      LEADSHUG_E2E_DATABASE_URL: postgresql://channels_validation:channels_validation_only@validation-postgres:5432/leadshug_timeline_channels_ci?schema=public
      LEADSHUG_API_PORT: "3100"
      LEADSHUG_FIXTURE_PORT: "3101"
      SESSION_SECRET: validation-session-secret-only
```

```
  "severity": "High CVSS3.1 7.5",
```
